Use this form for automatic rollover of mandatory distributions to individual retirement accounts. For mandatory distributions payable directly to the participant, use the Withdrawal - Mandatory Distributions Paid Directly to the Participant form.\n. If the participant address provided below is new or different than what is currently on record with John Hancock we will update our records accordingly. Ensure your next census submission includes revised employee information to avoid your file superseding the information supplied on this form.\n· A Form 1099R will be issued for each distribution and loan default, if applicable, by January 31 of the following year.\n. This request is subject to the processing and procedure guidelines contained in John Hancock's Administrative Guidelines for Financial Transactions (\"AGFT\"). The latest AGFT is available on the John Hancock plan sponsor website, or you may contact your John Hancock representative for a copy.\nAll changes must be initialed in pen (including numbers crossed out or changed using correction fluid).\nCompleted documents must be submitted on the website using the Submit a Document tool. For further assistance, please contact your Client Account Representative.\n1. General Information\nThe Trustee of JOCOR ENTERPRISES, LLC 401(K) PROFIT SHARING\nPlan (\"the Plan\")\n103349\nContractholder Name\nContract Number\nTo request distributions for multiple participants, check the box below and ensure a Trustee/Authorized Signer has printed and signed their name in section 6 of this form. You must also complete the John Hancock provided template. Refer to the file layout located on the plan sponsor website for the required format. Ensure all fields are completed on the template. The completed form and template must be submitted on the website using the Submit a Document tool. If submitted by the trustee/authorized signer via Submit a Document, then the excel attachment requires their name but does not require a signature. :unselected: See attached spreadsheet\nNote: If there is an excessive number of transaction requests with respect to your plan and John Hancock is unable to complete the requests that are \"in good order\" on or prior to the next market day after receipt, we will notify you or your designated contact as soon as administratively practicable to advise of the anticipated timeline for processing all the transactions.\n2. If a loan is active at the time of distribution, the applicable age-dependent loan distribution code will apply and a separate 1099-R will be issued.\nIf the early distribution exception code applies, check here. :unselected: (Code 2 will be applied)\n3. Vesting\nVesting is mandatory. The unvested money will be forfeited using instructions given in the Employer Unvested Money section below.\nVesting Percentage(s)\n% for ALL Employer money types\nOR\nVesting varies by money type as indicated below\nMoney Type\n%\nER Match\nProfit Sharing\n20\nOther ER Money\n%\nOther ER Money\n%\nEmployer Unvested Money\nIf no box is selected below, direction for forfeitures previously provided to John Hancock will be applied to any unvested money in the participant's account. If no direction for forfeitures has been provided and no box is selected below, any unvested money will remain in the participant's account invested according to the current investment instructions.\nIf you determine the unvested portion of the account is not forfeitable, then you may wish to select leave in participant's account as invested so that the participant continues to have the ability to direct the investment of the full balance of his/her account (including any unvested money). :unselected: Pay outstanding John Hancock charges :unselected: Refund to Plan Trustee :selected: Transfer to Cash Account :unselected: Leave in Participant account and transfer to default fund :unselected: Leave in Participant account as invested\n4. I also certify that each of the participants listed on this form or on the attached spreadsheet(s) has failed to make an affirmative election either to have the distribution due paid to him or her directly in cash or paid in a direct rollover to an eligible retirement plan. content: "Manulife® Retirement
John Hancock®
Reset Form
Withdrawal - Automatic Rollover of Mandatory Distributions to Individual Retirement Plans
Important Information about this Form
. Use this form for automatic rollover of mandatory distributions to individual retirement accounts. For mandatory distributions payable directly to the participant, use the Withdrawal - Mandatory Distributions Paid Directly to the Participant form.
. If the participant address provided below is new or different than what is currently on record with John Hancock we will update our records accordingly. Ensure your next census submission includes revised employee information to avoid your file superseding the information supplied on this form.
· A Form 1099R will be issued for each distribution and loan default, if applicable, by January 31 of the following year.
. This request is subject to the processing and procedure guidelines contained in John Hancock's Administrative Guidelines for Financial Transactions ("AGFT"). The latest AGFT is available on the John Hancock plan sponsor website, or you may contact your John Hancock representative for a copy.
All changes must be initialed in pen (including numbers crossed out or changed using correction fluid).
Completed documents must be submitted on the website using the Submit a Document tool. For further assistance, please contact your Client Account Representative.
1. General Information
The Trustee of JOCOR ENTERPRISES, LLC 401(K) PROFIT SHARING
Plan ("the Plan")
103349
Contractholder Name
Contract Number
To request distributions for multiple participants, check the box below and ensure a Trustee/Authorized Signer has printed and signed their name in section 6 of this form. You must also complete the John Hancock provided template. Refer to the file layout located on the plan sponsor website for the required format. Ensure all fields are completed on the template. The completed form and template must be submitted on the website using the Submit a Document tool. If submitted by the trustee/authorized signer via Submit a Document, then the excel attachment requires their name but does not require a signature. :unselected: See attached spreadsheet
Note: If there is an excessive number of transaction requests with respect to your plan and John Hancock is unable to complete the requests that are "in good order" on or prior to the next market day after receipt, we will notify you or your designated contact as soon as administratively practicable to advise of the anticipated timeline for processing all the transactions.
2. Participant Information

Has the final contribution been submitted for this participant? If the final payroll for this participant has not been submitted to John Hancock, provide the final payroll ending date:
Month Day Year
If a date is provided, John Hancock will coordinate processing of this distribution with receipt of the final payroll to avoid additional contribution payouts that often remain uncashed.
In-Plan Roth Rollover (IRR) Assets
For a total withdrawal, we will report the original rollover amount processed as the amount allocable to IRR assets.
Note: All Roth assets held by the participant would need to be taken into consideration when calculating the amount allocable to the IRR.
IRS Distribution Code
The IRS distribution code G will be used. If a loan is active at the time of distribution, the applicable age-dependent loan distribution code will apply and a separate 1099-R will be issued.
If the early distribution exception code applies, check here. :unselected: (Code 2 will be applied)

If no box is selected below, direction for forfeitures previously provided to John Hancock will be applied to any unvested money in the participant's account. If no direction for forfeitures has been provided and no box is selected below, any unvested money will remain in the participant's account invested according to the current investment instructions.
If you determine the unvested portion of the account is not forfeitable, then you may wish to select leave in participant's account as invested so that the participant continues to have the ability to direct the investment of the full balance of his/her account (including any unvested money). :unselected: Pay outstanding John Hancock charges :unselected: Refund to Plan Trustee :selected: Transfer to Cash Account :unselected: Leave in Participant account and transfer to default fund :unselected: Leave in Participant account as invested

5. Third Party Administrator (TPA) Withdrawal Fee
Complete this section to direct John Hancock to pay the fee indicated below from the participant's account balance to the Third Party Administrator currently on record for the Contract. No fee will be applied if this section is not completed.
The fee will be deducted from the participant's account balance at the time of the distribution using the standard withdrawal protocol and will be held in John Hancock's general business account until paid to the TPA.

6. Trustee/Authorized Signer Signature
As Trustee/Authorized Signer, I certify, under penalties of perjury, that based on the plan sponsor's record, (i) the name shown on this form and its attachments, if any, is the legal name of the participant; (ii) the number shown on this form is the correct taxpayer identification number (Social Security Number) of the participant; and, (iii) the participant is a U.S. person (including a U.S. resident alien) unless indicated otherwise above. I acknowledge that John Hancock will rely on this certification in determining the tax withholding and reporting requirements applicable to the requested distribution and agree to hold John Hancock harmless for any errors made in reliance upon this certification.
I hereby authorize John Hancock to rely and act upon the instructions provided on this form and its attachments, if any. I understand that it is my responsibility to ensure that the withdrawal(s) requested herein are permitted by law and, if applicable, consistent with the terms of the Plan. If the amount withdrawn is paid directly to the Plan Trustee, I also agree and acknowledge that I am responsible for the proper handling of the funds in accordance with the requirements of the law.
I also certify that, under the terms of the Plan, the distribution due to each participant listed under Section 2 or on the attached spreadsheet(s) above is required to be paid in a direct rollover to an IRA/Roth IRA. I further certify that all necessary information required to be furnished to each such participant, including an explanation of the Plan's automatic rollover provisions and information regarding the IRA/Roth IRA provider(s) and the fees and expenses attendant to the IRA/Roth IRA, as well as the tax rules required by IRC Section 402(f), have been sent to each such participant. I also certify that each of the participants listed on this form or on the attached spreadsheet(s) has failed to make an affirmative election either to have the distribution due paid to him or her directly in cash or paid in a direct rollover to an eligible retirement plan. I also certify that, if applicable, the Withholding Certificate for Pension or Annuity Payments (Form W-4P) for the states of Michigan or lowa have been properly obtained, completed in accordance with Michigan and Iowa law, and that any amount exempt from state tax withholding described above accurately reflects such Withholding Certificate submitted by the Participant(s).

I understand that this fee will be deducted from the participant's account balance at the time of the distribution using standard withdrawal protocol and will be held in the general business account of John Hancock until paid to the Third Party Administrator. I hereby represent that this fee is in accordance with the fee schedule that has been approved by the plan's trustee or named fiduciary as reasonable and authorized under the terms of the plan.
